Projection to simplex implementation:
def project_simplex(v):
    # v array
    if v.size==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,len+1)) > (cssv-1))[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
Sum to 1. For scale invariant, projection to sum1. We can use.

But optimizing over positive simplex with projection may set many zeros. That's allowed. Zeros can be okay.

Pseudo:

def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, max_steps=200000, use_adam=True, verbose=True, tag=""):
    n = len(p0)
    p = np.asarray(p0, float); p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0); p=np.clip(p,0,1000); s=p.sum(); if s<1e-12: p=np.ones(n); s=n; p/=s
    L = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=2*n*max(_convolve_fft_arr(p,L)); best_step=0
    # Adam states
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    b1=0.9; b2=0.999
    # Schedule
    total? Unknown exact time; we can use elapsed and estimated? We can schedule based on iterations; but time limit. Use lr decay by it count over max_steps. Or time-based: beta = max(0, 1 - elapsed/ (deadline - start - 10)). Since we don't know total, use progress = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1, min(max_steps*est_time, deadline-start-?))). Simpler use iteration progress over max_steps set according to n.
    max_steps depending on n: if n<=384: 250000; elif <=768:150000; elif <=1536:80000; else 40000. But time budget may shorter; elapsed break.
    # soft start: 0.5 down to 0.02.
    st_time=0.0? c varies; soft_frac = 0.35 * (1-progress) + 0.02*progress.
    lr = 0.05*(1+cos(pi*progress))*0.5 +0.002.
    # Use Adam? We'll implement optional.
    for it in range(max_steps):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       P = fft(rfft?) We can use np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       c = irfft(P*P,L)[:2n-1]; if any negative small: c=np.maximum(c,0)
       maxc = c.max()
       score = 2*n*maxc
       if score < best_score - 1e-15: update
       T = soft_frac * maxc
       if T <= 1e-15: choose hard direct? maybe T=1e-12
       # weights:
       if use_hard (soft_frac < 0.03): find top_k indices maybe 1? Let's use direct for k=argmax and maybe top2.
           k=int(np.argmax(c))
           h=np.zeros(n)
           # add vectorized
           lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
           # j indices k-i for i lo..hi: from k-lo down to k-hi
           h[lo:hi+1] = 2.0 * p[k-lo : k-hi-1 : -1]? Need construct.
           # Example k=3,n=5: lo=0 hi=3, j:3,2,1,0. p[3:-1:-1]
           # If k-hi=0 -> k-lo: -1:-1
           h[lo:hi+1] = 2.0 * p[k - lo : k - hi - 1 : -1]
           # If k-hi == -? hi=min(n-1,k). If k<n, hi=k, k-hi=0 -> good slice k-lo:-1:-1.
           # If k≥n, hi=n-1, k-hi≥1? slice k-lo:k-hi-1:-1; if k-hi-1 may be -? If k-hi=0? occurs when k=n-1? hi=n-1 -> k-hi=0, slice k-lo:-1:-1. In negative case if k-hi<0? no.
           # General safe: js = np.arange(k-hi, k-lo+1)? But let's vector.
       else:
           # Normalize weights: shift to max zero
           ts = np.minimum((c - maxc) / T, 0.0)
           w = np.exp(ts)  # max 1 at peak
           # Because sum w maybe large; normalize? Gradient scaling can be part of lr. Normalize to sum1 to keep h sum1. w /= w.sum()
           W = zeros(L); W[:2n-1]=w
           H = irfft(fft(W)*conj(P), L)
           h[0]=H[0]; h[1:]=H[L-n+1:L][::-1]
           # maybe multiply by 2? not necessary scale.
       # subtract mean to reduce constant
       h = h - np.mean(h)
       # Adam
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*h
       v = b2*v + (1-b2)*h*h
       mhat = m/(1-b1**(it+1)); vhat=v/(1-b2**(it+1))
       step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
       p_new = p - step
       # If p_new has nonfinite or bad sum, reduce step
       p_new = np.maximum(p_new, 0.0)
       s = p_new.sum()
       if not finite or s<1e-12: p_new=p*0.9? fallback p_new=p - 0.1*lr*...; maybe simply break?
       p_new /= s
       p = p_new
       # Periodic perturbation
       if (it+1) % 2000 == 0 or no improve 3000:
           if do_perturb(p): maybe
    return best,best_score

Perturbation function:
def perturb_p(p, rng, strength=0.3):
   n=len(p)
   q=p.astype(float).copy()
   # Choose random proportion, e.g., np.maximum(0.05, 1.0/n)
   m = max(16, n//10)
   idx = rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)
   factors = rng.uniform(0.5, 1.5, size=m)
   q[idx] *= factors
   q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s>0: q/=s
   return q

LP function details:
def lp_best_response(p, time_limit=10):
   n=len(p). if n>768 return None (maybe 1024 too slow? Let's set 768; can try 1024 if time). Use dense A if n<=512 else maybe skip to avoid build. But we can build sparse for 1024? Let's keep dense n<=512 for safe. The best length likely 512/1024. If best length 1024, LP skipped maybe. We can build sparse for 1024 if we want. Let's implement dense for n<=600 and sparse optional? Simpler dense for n<=512; for 1024 use scipy.sparse to reduce memory. But dense 2047x1025 ~2M floats=16MB, not bad. Build loops 2047 rows okay. Could allow n<=1024 dense. 16MB fine. For 1536, 4095x1537 ~6.3M=50MB, maybe okay but slower. Set max 1024. Use linprog time_limit maybe 10s.
   Build A = np.empty((2n-1,n+1)); A[:,0] = -1.0.
   Use for m in range(2*n-1):
      lo=max(0,m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m); length=hi-lo+1
      # Need assign p[m-lo : m-hi-1 : -1]
      stop = m-hi-1
      A[m, 1+lo:1+hi+1] = p[m-lo:stop:-1]
   A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,1:]=1.0
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[0]=1.0
   b_ub=np.zeros(2n-1)
   from scipy.optimize import linprog
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=[(0,None)]*(n+1), method='highs', options={'time_limit': time_limit, 'disp': False})
   if res.success or res.status in (0,2?)? HiGHS status 0 optimal, 2 time limit? We can accept if res.x available finite. g=res.x[1:]; s=g.sum(); if s>0: g=g/s; return g.
   else None.

def try_blend(p, g, best_score, L):

Now, let's think of a more mathematical improvement: Use iterative reweighted least squares / Lagrange multiplier from active set to solve a quadratic subproblem. At local optimum, for active lags K with weights w summing1, h_i constant on support. We can approximate current gradient h and project to constant by moving p. Multiplicative does that. But we can directly solve for p such that h_i (computed from active weights) constant, i.e., p is approximate eigenvector of a convolution operator. Given active weights w and current p? The operator T_w p = H. We want H constant. We update p multiplicatively to make H constant. This is similar to Power iteration: p ← exp(-η H)/sum. We are doing that with H from c-weighted. Could enhance by choosing w to maximize the constant? The weights are softmax of c to satisfy KKT. Good.

Maybe we can use "active set stabilization": After several iterations, identify top coefficients where c > (1 - delta)*maxc, set weights only there uniformly (or exponential). - _resample(base,n): if len(base)==0 return ones; if len(base)==1 return normalizing [base?]; x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(base)); y=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), x_old, base); return _normalize(y,1.0)

def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, max_steps=None, tag="", lr_start=0.5, lr_end=0.02, soft_start=0.35, soft_end=0.005, perturb=True, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(12345+n)
    p = _clean_array(p0).astype(float).copy()
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return None, inf
    p=_normalize(p,1.0)
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_sequence(p) (or compute P initially)
    if max_steps is None:
       if n<=384: max_steps=500000
       elif n<=768: 300000
       elif n<=1536: 150000
       else: 80000
    t0=time.monotonic()
    plan_time=max(1.0, float(deadline - t0))
    # initialize P,c
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
    if not finite: c=...
    maxc=float(c.max()); score=2n*maxc
    # bias correction counters
    b1=0.85; b2? We only use momentum on grad_dim, no adaptive. m=np.zeros(n)
    lr_scale=1.0
    last_best_time=t0; last_best_it=0; bad_count=0
    for it in range(max_steps):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5: break
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
       progress=min(1.0, elapsed/plan_time)
       # if max_steps low, also progress2=(it+1)/max_steps; use max? Use progress = max(progress, (it+1)/max_steps)? If iters end before time, we want end schedule. Use progress = max(elapsed/plan_time, (it+1)/max_steps). But if max_steps high, no.
       it_progress=(it+1)/float(max_steps)
       progress=max(progress, it_progress)
       # But if plan_time long and max_steps reached, progress=1.
       # lr and soft
       lr_base = lr_end + 0.5*(lr_start-lr_end)*(1.0+math.cos(math.pi*progress)) * lr_scale
       soft = soft_end + (soft_start-soft_end)*(1.0-progress)
       # weights
       maxc = float(c.max())
       if maxc <= 0: break
       score=2*n*maxc
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best=p.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic(); last_best_it=it
       T = soft * maxc
       # If T very small use active threshold?
       if T <= 1e-15:
          # one-hot or top equal max
          mask = c >= maxc - 1e-12*maxc? use 1e-12
       else:
          ex=(c-maxc)/T
          ex=np.minimum(ex,0.0)
          # If ex too negative, exp underflow safe. To avoid all underflow if maxc? max ex 0 => at least one 1.
          w=np.exp(ex)
          ws=w.sum()
          if ws<=0 or not finite: w=np.zeros(2n-1); w[int(c.argmax())]=1; ws=1
       # else normalize w
       w=w/ws maybe
       # gradient H
       W=np.zeros(L); W[:2*n-1]=w
       H=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(W,L) * np.conj(P), L)
       h=H[:n].astype(float)
       h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
       hs=h.sum()
       if hs <= 1e-15:
          h=np.full(n,1.0/n)
       else:
          h/=hs
       # natural gradient
       nat = p * h
       ns = nat.sum()
       if ns > 1e-15:
          g=nat/ns
       else:
          g=h
       gdim = n * g - 1.0
       # sum should zero but fix
       gdim = gdim - gdim.mean()
       # momentum
       m = b1*m + (1.0-b1)*gdim
       # bias correction not needed? Use t:
       bias = 1.0 - b1**(it+1)
       mhat = m / bias
       # clip
       mhat = np.clip(mhat, -3.0, 8.0)
       # update
       exponent = -lr_base * mhat
       exponent = np.clip(exponent, -50.0, 50.0) # safe
       q = p * np.exp(exponent)
       q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       s=q.sum()
       if not finite or s<=1e-15:
          q=best.copy()
          lr_scale=max(0.2, lr_scale*0.5)
       else:
          q/=s
          # optional zero tiny
          # if (it+1)%5000==0: q[q<1e-14]=0; s=...; q/=s
          # compute new P,c
          P=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
          c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]
          c=np.where(c<0,0,c) maybe if small; but for weights max unaffected. To avoid negative in softmax ex? c negative about -1e-16, maxc positive; ex negative large; okay. But if maxc negative? no. Use c=np.maximum(c,0) to safe.
          new_score=2*n*float(c.max())
          p=q
          # check catastrophic
          if new_score > score * 2.0 + 1e-9:
             # revert partially? set p=old? We already changed P/c. Could keep? Maybe revert if too bad.
             p=best.copy()
             P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=...
             lr_scale=max(0.3, lr_scale*0.7)
             m=np.zeros(n)
       # perturbation
       if perturb and ((it - last_best_it) > max(2000, n) or (time.monotonic()-last_best_time)>5.0):
          # with some probability
          if rng.random() < 0.7:
             q = _perturb_mull(p, rng, sigma=0.4, frac=0.2)

   cands=[]
   for n in lengths:
      # uniform maybe alloc 40
      p=np.ones(n); cands.append((100, p, 60))
      # beta right
      for beta in [0.25,0.5,1.0]:
          p=_beta_sequence(n,beta,False); cands.append((200,p,60))
          p=_beta_sequence(n,beta,True); cands.append((210,p,60))
      # step
      p=_step_sequence(n,0.5,2.0,False); cands.append((300,p,50))
      p=_step_sequence(n,0.5,1.5,True); ...
      # gauss shifted left
      p=_gauss_sequence(n,0.25,0.18); cands.append((350,p,50))
      p=_gauss_sequence(n,0.70,0.18); ...
      # smooth noise
      p=_smooth_noise_sequence(n,rng); cands.append((400,p,50))
   return cands

res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit':float(time_limit),'presolve':True,'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-6?})

   x=np.asarray(res.x); if finite: g=x[1:]; s=g.sum(); if s>0: return g/s
return None

Now, code for _smooth_noise_sequence:
def _smooth_noise_sequence(n,rng):
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
   val=np.ones(n)
   for f in (1,2,3,4,5):
       amp=1.0/f
       phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi))
       val += amp*np.sin(2*math.pi*f*x/n + phase)
   val=np.maximum(val,0.05)
   return _normalize(val,1.0)
Could produce asymmetric.

_next_pow2:
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
Check m=1 ->1, m=2 -> (1).bit_length=1 ->2, m=3 -> (2).bit_length=2 ->4. Good.

_clean_array(x):
def _clean_array(x):
    try: arr=np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    except: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if arr.ndim==0: arr=arr.reshape(1)
    else: arr=arr.ravel()
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    return arr

_normalize(a,target=1.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size; if n==0: return a
   s=float(a.sum()); if not finite or s<=1e-300:
       if target>0: return np.full(n,target/float(n)) else zeros
   return a*(target/s)

_score_sequence(p):
   p=np.asarray(p,float); n=p.size; if n==0: return inf
   L=_next_pow2(2*n-1); P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0.0); if c.size==0 return inf; return float(2*n*np.max(c))
_score_exact(p): c=np.convolve(p,p,mode='full'); return float(2*n*np.max(c))

_resample:
def _resample(base,n):
   base=_clean_array(base); m=base.size; if m==0: return np.ones(n)
   if m==1: return np.full(n,1.0)
   if m==n: return _normalize(base.copy(),1.0)
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,base)
   y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
   y=_normalize(y,1.0)
   return y

_beta, step, gauss, noise as above.
_step_sequence:
   m=max(1,int(round(a_frac*n)))
   if m>=n: m=n-1
   p=np.ones(n)
   if not flip: p[:m]=ratio
   else: p[m:]=ratio
   return _normalize(p,1.0)

_smooth_noise:
   x=np.arange(n)
   val=np.ones(n)
   for f in range(1,6): amp=1.0/f; phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)); val += amp*np.sin(2*math.pi*f*x/n + phase)
   val=np.maximum(val,0.05); return _normalize(val,1.0)

_perturb_mull(p,rng,sigma=0.4,frac=0.25):
   n=p.size; q=p.copy()
   k=max(1,int(frac*n))
   idx=rng.choice(n,size=k,replace=False)
   # factors lognormal
   logf = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=k)
   logf = np.clip(logf, -3, 3)
   q[idx] *= np.exp(logf)
   q=np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s>0: q=q/s
   return q

_lp_best_response(p, time_limit=8.0):
   if not _HAVE_LINPROG: return None
   n=p.size
   if n<8 or n>1024: return None
   pb = p + 1e-7
   s=pb.sum(); if s<=0: return None; pb=pb/s
   rows=2*n-1; cols=n+1
   A=np.zeros((rows,cols), dtype=np.float64)
   A[:,0] = -1.0
   for m in range(rows):
       lo = 0 if m < n-1? formula:
       lo=max(0, m-n+1); hi=min(n-1,m)
       stop = m - hi - 1
       st = m - lo
       if stop < 0:
           A[m,1+lo:1+hi+1] = pb[st:-1:-1]
       else:
           A[m,1+lo:1+hi+1] = pb[st:stop:-1]
   A_eq=np.zeros((1,cols)); A_eq[0,1:]=1.0
   c_obj=np.zeros(cols); c_obj[0]=1.0
   b_ub=np.zeros(rows)
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*cols
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit':float(time_limit),'presolve':True})
   except Exception:
      return None
   if res is None or not hasattr(res,'x') or res.x is None: return None
   x=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
   if x.size < n+1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return None
   g=x[1:]
   gs=float(g.sum())
   if gs<=0 or not finite: return None
   g=np.maximum(g,0.0); gs=g.sum(); if gs<=0: return None
   return g/gs

_pair_polish:
def _pair_polish(p, deadline, rng, tag=""):
   p=_normalize(_clean_array(p),1.0).copy()
   n=p.size
   if n<8: return p, _score_sequence(p)
   L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_sequence(p)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
   t0=time.monotonic(); no_improve=0; trail=0
   src_count=max(16, n//16)
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0:
       trail +=1
       maxc=float(c.max()); cur_score=2*n*maxc
       if no_improve > 2000: break
       # top K active
       mlen=2*n-1
       K=min(8, mlen)
       if K==mlen:
          top=np.arange(mlen)
       else:
          top=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       h=np.zeros(n)
       # option weights uniform? Could weight by c rank? Use all 1; normalize.
       for kk in top:
           k=int(kk)
           lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
           if hi < lo: continue
           if hi == lo:
               h[lo] += p[k-lo]
           else:
               end = k - hi
               if end == 0:
                   h[lo:hi+1] += p[k-lo::-1]
               else:
                   h[lo:hi+1] += p[k-lo:end-1:-1]
       hs=h.sum()
       if hs<=0: continue
       h/=hs
       nat=p*h; ns=nat.sum(); if ns<=0: nat=h; else: nat=nat/ns
       # source high, target low
       if n > 2*src_count:
          src=np.argpartition(nat, -src_count)[-src_count:]
          tgt=np.argpartition(nat, src_count)[:]
       else:
          src=np.argsort(nat)[-src_count:]
          tgt=np.argsort(nat)[:src_count]
       if src.size==0 or tgt.size==0: continue
       u=int(rng.choice(src)); v=int(rng.choice(tgt))
       if u==v: continue
       base_move=p[u]*float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
       improved=False
       for frac in (0.25,1.0,0.5,0.75,0.15):
           move=base_move*frac
           if move <=0 or move > p[u]: continue
           q=p.copy()
           q[u]-=move; q[v]+=move
           q=np.maximum(q,0)
           qs=_score_sequence(q)
           if qs < best_score - 1e-12:
               best=q; best_score=qs; p=q; P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0); improved=True; no_improve=0; break
       if not improved:
           no_improve +=1
       if trail % 200 ==0:
           print(f"[_pair_polish {tag}] n={n} best={best_score:.7f} trail={trail}")
   return best,best_score
This recomputes _score_sequence for each fraction (5 per trial) heavy. But deadline maybe short. Could limit trials by time. It may be too slow if n=2048 and 30s: Each _score_sequence 2 FFT; 500 trials *5*2=5000 FFT length4096 ~ maybe 10s; okay. But h top direct loop 8*2048 ~16k per trial, 500 trials 8M, fine.
However if it runs, it may not improve and burn time. We'll call only if remaining >30 and best length <=1024? Maybe if time large. Use after LP? We'll include with sub_deadline min(60, remaining*0.25).

Could pair_polish move mass to v with zero nat but high p? If v has high p, target low nat may still have moderate p; okay. It doesn't preserve c? sum preserved.

Now, optimize_sequence function. Let's write carefully.

def optimize_sequence(p0, deadline, max_steps=None, tag="", rng=None, lr_start=0.45, lr_end=0.015, soft_start=0.35, soft_end=0.004, perturb=True):
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(123456)
   p=_normalize(_clean_array(p0),1.0).astype(float).copy()
   n=p.size
   if n==0: return p, inf
   L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_sequence(p)
   if max_steps is None: ... based on n and maybe (deadline - now): max_steps large
   t0=time.monotonic(); plan_time=max(1.0, float(deadline - t0))
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
   m_mom=np.zeros(n)
   b1=0.85
   lr_scale=1.0; bad_count=0; last_best_time=t0; last_best_it=0; last_perturb_it=-100000; last_print=t0
   if n <= 1: return best,best_score
   print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} start={best_score:.7f} budget={plan_time:.1f}s")
   for it in range(max_steps):
      if time.monotonic() > deadline - 0.5: break
      elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
      progress_t=elapsed/plan_time if plan_time>1e-9 else 1.0
      progress_i=(it+1)/float(max_steps)
      progress=max(1.0, min? We want as both approach. Use progress = max(progress_t, progress_i) but if one reaches1 early, hard schedule. If max_steps high, progress_i small. If time stops, break. If max_steps lower, progress_i reaches1 at end. Good.
      progress = min(1.0, max(progress_t, progress_i))
      lr_base = (lr_end + 0.5*(lr_start-lr_end)*(1.0+math.cos(math.pi*progress))) * lr_scale
      if lr_base < 1e-4: lr_base=1e-4
      soft = soft_end + (soft_start-soft_end)*(1.0-progress)
      maxc=float(c.max())
      if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 1e-300:
          break
      score=2.0*n*maxc
      if score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=score; best=p.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic(); last_best_it=it
          if time.monotonic() - last_print > 5.0:
              print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} it={it} score={best_score:.7f} lr={lr_base:.4g} soft={soft:.4g}"); last_print=time.monotonic()
      # weights
      T=soft*maxc
      wlen=2*n-1
      if T <= 1e-15*maxc or not np.isfinite(T):
          w=np.zeros(wlen); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
      else:
          ex=(c-maxc)/T
          ex=np.minimum(ex,0.0)
          ex=np.maximum(ex,-50.0)
          w=np.exp(ex)
          ws=float(w.sum())
          if ws<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
              w=np.zeros(wlen); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
          else:
              w=w/ws
      # gradient
      W=np.zeros(L); W[:wlen]=w
      H=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(W,L)*np.conj(P), L)
      h=H[:n]
      h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
      hs=float(h.sum())
      if hs<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(hs):
          h=np.full(n,1.0/n)
      else:
          h=h/hs
      nat=p*h
      ns=float(nat.sum())
      if ns<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ns):
          g=h
      else:
          g=nat/ns
      gdim=n*g - 1.0
      gdim=gdim - float(np.mean(gdim))
      m_mom = b1*m_mom + (1.0-b1)*gdim
      bias=1.0 - b1**(it+1)
      if bias <= 0: bias=1e-12
      mhat=m_mom / bias
      mhat=np.clip(mhat, -3.0, 8.0)
      exponent = -lr_base * mhat
      exponent = np.clip(exponent, -50.0, 50.0)
      q=p*np.exp(exponent)
      q=np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
      s=float(q.sum())
      old_score=score
      if s<=1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
          q=best.copy(); lr_scale=max(0.3, lr_scale*0.5)
      else:
          q=q/s
          P=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
          c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:wlen]
          c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
          nmax=float(c.max())
          new_score=2.0*n*nmax
          p=q
          if new_score < best_score - 1e-12:
              best_score=new_score; best=p.copy(); last_best_time=time.monotonic(); last_best_it=it
              if time.monotonic() - last_print > 5.0:
                  print(...); last_print=...
          if new_score > old_score * 1.05 and new_score > old_score + 1e-9:
              bad_count += 1
          else:
              bad_count = max(0,bad_count-1)
          if bad_count > 200:
              p=best.copy()
              P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:wlen]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
              bad_count=0; lr_scale=max(0.4, lr_scale*0.7); m_mom=np.zeros(n)
      # perturbation
      if perturb and (it - last_perturb_it >= max(1500, n) and (it - last_best_it > max(2500, n) or (time.monotonic()-last_best_time) > 8.0)):
          if rng.random() < 0.8:
              last_perturb_it=it
              q2=_perturb_mull(p, rng, sigma=0.45, frac=0.25)
              qs2=_score_sequence(q2)
              cur_score=2.0*n*float(c.max())
              accept=False
              if qs2 < best_score * (1.0 + 1e-7): accept=True
              elif qs2 < cur_score * 1.15 and rng.random() < 0.25: accept=True
              if accept:
                  p=_normalize(q2,1.0).copy()
                  P=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:wlen]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
                  m_mom=np.zeros(n); lr_scale=1.0; bad_count=0
                  print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} perturb accept score={qs2:.7f}")
   # end loop, if current better update
   try:
      cur_score=2.0*n*float(c.max())
      if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
          best_score=cur_score; best=p.copy()
   except: pass
   print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} end best={best_score:.7f}")
   return best, best_score

Potential bug: In loop, at top we use c from previous update. If after update we reverted to best and recomputed c, ok. If after perturbation accepted and updated c, ok. If new_score worsens but not bad_count revert, c new. Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: budget_s=float(budget_s)
    except: budget_s=2380.0
    if budget_s < 60: budget_s = max(60,budget_s)
    total = budget_s - 10.0
    reserve = max(120.0, min(420.0, total*0.2))
    exploration_end = t0 + total - reserve
    eval_deadline = t0 + total - 2.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    base=None
    try:
       arr=_clean_array(height_sequence_1)
       if arr.size>=8: base=arr
    except Exception: base=None
    best_seq=np.ones(256); best_score=float('_score_sequence(best_seq)')? If base unavailable, maybe 2. But we use inf.
    best_seq=None; best_score=inf
    cands=_make_candidates(base,rng)
    for name,p0,alloc in cands:
        if time.monotonic() > exploration_end - 5.0: break
        remaining_alloc = exploration_end - time.monotonic()
        if remaining_alloc < 15: break
        a=min(alloc, remaining_alloc)
        p0=_normalize(p0,1.0)
        s0=_score_sequence(p0)
        print(f"Trying {name} n={p0.size} start={s0:.7f} alloc={a:.1f}s")
        if s0 < best_score:
            best_seq=_normalize(p0,1.0).copy(); best_score=s0
        # Maybe skip if s0 much worse and alloc small? no.
        d = time.monotonic() + a
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(p0, d, tag=name, rng=rng)
        if score < best_score:
            best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=score
            print(f"New global best from {name}: {best_score:.7f} n={len(best_seq)}")
        else:
            print(f"{name} best={score:.7f} global={best_score:.7f}")
    # Final refinement
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq=np.ones(256); best_score=2.0
    else:
        best_seq=_normalize(best_seq,1.0).copy()
    remaining = eval_deadline - time.monotonic()
    # final hard polish
    if remaining > 60:
        dmin = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(300.0, remaining*0.35))
        # If we want use more but keep reserve. dmin=min(dmin, remaining*0.6?) Use min(remaining*0.45, 350)
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(best_seq, dmin, tag='final-polish', rng=rng, lr_start=0.3, lr_end=0.006, soft_start=0.15, soft_end=0.0008, perturb=True)
        if score < best_score: best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=score
    # upscale
    n_best=best_seq.size
    if best_seq is not None and n_best < 2048 and time.monotonic() < eval_deadline - 90:
        nn = min(2048, n_best*2)
        p_up = _resample(best_seq, nn)
        rem2 = eval_deadline - time.monotonic()
        d2 = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(300.0, rem2*0.45))
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(p_up, d2, tag=f'upscale-{nn}', rng=rng, lr_start=0.35, lr_end=0.008, soft_start=0.2, soft_end=0.001, perturb=True)
        if score < best_score:
            best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=score
            print(f"Upscale improved to {best_score:.7f} n={nn}")
    # If best length >512 and time left, maybe downsample to half? Could be lower? Unlikely but maybe discrete.
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size > 1024 and time.monotonic() < eval_deadline - 60:
        nn=max(512, best_seq.size//2)
        p_down=_resample(best_seq, nn)
        d3=time.monotonic()+max(20.0,min(120.0,(eval_deadline-time.monotonic())*0.4))
        seq, score=optimize_sequence(p_down,d3,tag='downs',rng=rng,...)
        if score<best_score: update
    # LP polish
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < eval_deadline - 20:
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > eval_deadline - 15: break
            rem_time = (eval_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.2
            tl = max(3.0, min(10.0, rem_time))
            g=_lp_best_response(best_seq, tl)
            if g is None: break
            improved=False
            for mix in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5):
                q=(1.0-mix)*best_seq + mix*g
                q=np.maximum(q,0.0); q=_normalize(q,1.0)
                qs=_score_sequence(q)
                if qs < best_score:
                    best_seq=q.copy(); best_score=qs; improved=True; break
            if not improved: break
    # pair polish
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < eval_deadline - 35:
        rem = eval_deadline - time.monotonic()
        d4=time.monotonic()+max(20.0, min(80.0, rem*0.3))
        seq,score=_pair_polish(best_seq, d4, rng, tag='pair')
        if score < best_score:
            best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=score
    # final small polish
    if time.monotonic() < eval_deadline - 20:
        d5=time.monotonic()+max(10.0, min(60.0,(eval_deadline-time.monotonic())*0.8))
        seq,score=optimize_sequence(best_seq,d5,tag='final2',rng=rng, lr_start=0.15, lr_end=0.003, soft_start=0.1,soft_end=0.0004,perturb=False)
        if score<best_score: update
    # final exact
    n=best_seq.size
    target_sum = max(1.0, math.sqrt(2.0*float(n)))
    final=_normalize(best_seq,target_sum)
    final=np.clip(final,0.0,1000.0)
    # ensure no negative zeros
    out=[float(x) for x in final]
    try:
       es=evaluate_sequence(out)
       if np.isfinite(es):
          print(f"Final evaluate_sequence: {es:.7f} n={n}")
       else:
          print("Final evaluate_sequence inf")
    except Exception as e:
       print("evaluate Sequence not available")
    es2=_score_exact(final)
    print(f"Final exact score {es2:.7f}")
    if not np.isfinite(es2) or es2 > 10.0:
        fallback=_normalize(np.ones(512), math.sqrt(1024))
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]
    return out

def _make_candidates(base, rng):
    cands=[]
    if base is not None:
        m=base.size
        nbase=min(m,2048)
        if m != nbase: p=_resample(base,nbase)
        else: p=_normalize(base.copy(),1.0)
        cands.append((f"base{nbase}", p, 300.0))
        # Upsample common, priority based on value? order list.
        for nn, alloc in ((1024,260.0),(2048,220.0),(1536,140.0),(768,100.0)):
            if nn != nbase and nn <= 2048 and m >= 8:
                cands.append((f"base{nn}", _resample(base,nn), alloc))
        # jitter
        pjit = p * (1.0 + 0.15*rng.normal(size=nbase))
        pjit=np.maximum(pjit,0.0); pjit=_normalize(pjit,1.0)
        cands.append(("basejit", pjit, 100.0))
        # alternate params limited
        for n in (512,1024):
            cands.append((f"altBeta{n}", _beta_sequence(n,0.5,False), 80.0))
            cands.append((f"altBetaR{n}", _beta_sequence(n,0.5,True), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"altStep{n}", _step_sequence(n,0.5,2.0,False), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"altNoise{n}", _smooth_noise_sequence(n,rng), 40.0))
    else:
        for n in (512,1024):
            cands.append((f"uni{n}", np.ones(n), 120.0))
            for beta in (0.25,0.5,1.0):
                cands.append((f"beta{n}{beta}", _beta_sequence(n,beta,False), 80.0))
                cands.append((f"beta{n}r{beta}", _beta_sequence(n,beta,True), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"step{n}", _step_sequence(n,0.5,2.0,False), 70.0))
            cands.append((f"step{n}r", _step_sequence(n,0.5,1.5,True), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"gauss{n}l", _gauss_sequence(n,0.25,0.18), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"gauss{n}r", _gauss_sequence(n,0.70,0.18), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"noise{n}", _smooth_noise_sequence(n,rng), 50.0))
    return cands

Now, in candidate loop, if optimize_sequence returns best local (which may include initial p0 if no improvement). We update global. But if global best already much better, we still spend allocation on poor start. Could skip if s0 > max(best_score*1.5, 3.0) and allocation small? To save time, maybe if s0 > best_score + 0.2 and best_score finite, reduce alloc or skip. But good starts from base should improve. For param random, maybe not. We can add:
if best_score < inf and s0 > best_score * 1.05 + 0.2: alloc *=0.5
But not necessary. Could waste. Let's implement simple: if best_score finite and s0 - best_score > 0.5: alloc = min(alloc, 30.0) for n<=1024 else 20. This prevents wasting on very bad starts after good global. Use in candidate loop.
Need avoid closure. In propose:
if best_score < inf and s0 > best_score + 0.5:
    alloc = min(alloc, 30.0)
This is okay. If no best, no.

Now, if best_score after base ~1.517, alt param starts maybe ~1.7-2, difference >0.5? Not. They still. If s0 2.0, diff0.48, no. Good.

Now, final exact if best_score from internal sum1 maybe 1.517, output final exact maybe 1.517. Good.

Let's now final code. Use triple backticks python. Ensure no extra text. (The final answer should only code block.)
</think>

```python
"""
Searches for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The main experiment uses a multiplicative simplex descent on the peak
autoconvolution, with annealed soft-max weights, momentum, simulated
annealing-style perturbations, LP-based best-response blending, and
late-stage pair-transfer polishing.  It starts from the provided global
construction when available and also explores several analytic and
resampled initials of different lengths.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_LINPROG = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_LINPROG = False


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    """Smallest power of two >= m (m >= 1)."""
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_array(x):
    """Convert to a finite, non-negative 1-D float array clipped to [0, 1000]."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim == 0:
        arr = arr.reshape(1)
    else:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)


def _normalize(a, target: float = 1.0):
    """Normalize a non-negative array to have a given sum."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    s = float(a.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
        if target > 0:
            return np.full(n, target / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (target / s)


def _score_sequence(p):
    """
    Fast objective for an internally normalized sequence with sum 1:
        2 * n * max(convolve(p, p))
    If p is normalized, this is exactly the evaluator score.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[: 2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def _score_exact(p):
    """Exact evaluator objective for any non-negative sequence."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(p.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-30:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(p, p, mode="full")
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s))


def _resample(base, n: int):
    """Linearly resample a base sequence to length n and normalize to sum 1."""
    base = _clean_array(base)
    m = base.size
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * (1.0 / n)
    if m == 1:
        return _normalize(base, 1.0) * n if base[0] > 0 else np.ones(n)
    if m == n:
        return _normalize(base.copy(), 1.0)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, base)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    return _normalize(y, 1.0)


def _beta_sequence(n: int, beta: float, flip: bool = False):
    """One-sided power-law seed."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.5 / float(n), 1.0 - 0.5 / float(n), n)
    if flip:
        v = (1.0 - x) ** max(0.0, beta)
    else:
        v = x ** max(0.0, beta)
    v = np.maximum(v, 1e-6)
    return _normalize(v, 1.0)


def _step_sequence(n: int, a_frac: float, ratio: float, flip: bool = False):
    """Two-level step seed."""
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(max(1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    m = max(1, min(n - 1, int(round(a_frac * n))))
    p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if not flip:
        p[:m] = ratio
    else:
        p[m:] = ratio
    return _normalize(p, 1.0)


def _gauss_sequence(n: int, center: float, width: float):
    """Shifted Gaussian seed."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    v = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - center) / max(1e-6, width)) ** 2)
    v += 0.05
    return _normalize(v, 1.0)


def _smooth_noise_sequence(n: int, rng):
    """Low-frequency asymmetric positive seed."""
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for f in range(1, 6):
        amp = 1.0 / float(f)
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi))
        v += amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x / float(n) + phase)
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.05)
    return _normalize(v, 1.0)


def _perturb_mull(p, rng, sigma: float = 0.45, frac: float = 0.25):
    """Multiplicative random perturbation on a random subset of coordinates."""
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    q = p.copy()
    k = max(1, int(frac * n))
    idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
    logf = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=k)
    logf = np.clip(logf, -3.0, 3.0)
    q[idx] *= np.exp(logf)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    return _normalize(q, 1.0)


def _lp_best_response(p, time_limit: float = 8.0):
    """
    LP best response:
        min t
        s.t. (B * x)_k <= t for all k,
             sum x = 1, x >= 0.
    A tiny lift is added to p so that all columns are constrained.
    """
    if not _HAVE_LINPROG:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n < 8 or n > 1024:
        return None

    pb = p + 1e-7
    pb = _normalize(pb, 1.0)

    rows = 2 * n - 1
    cols = n + 1
    A = np.zeros((rows, cols), dtype=np.float64)
    A[:, 0] = -1.0

    for m in range(rows):
        lo = max(0, m - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, m)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        st = m - lo
        if hi == lo:
            A[m, 1 + lo] = pb[st]
        else:
            end = m - hi
            if end == 0:
                A[m, 1 + lo : 1 + hi + 1] = pb[st::-1]
            else:
                A[m, 1 + lo : 1 + hi + 1] = pb[st:end - 1:-1]

    A_eq = np.zeros((1, cols), dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq[0, 1:] = 1.0
    c_obj = np.zeros(cols, dtype=np.float64)
    c_obj[0] = 1.0
    b_ub = np.zeros(rows, dtype=np.float64)
    b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * cols

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": float(max(1.0, time_limit)), "presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size < n + 1 or (not np.all(np.isfinite(x))):
        return None
    g = np.maximum(x[1:], 0.0)
    gs = float(g.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(gs)) or gs <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return g / gs


def optimize_sequence(
    p0,
    deadline: float,
    max_steps: int = None,
    tag: str = "",
    rng=None,
    lr_start: float = 0.45,
    lr_end: float = 0.015,
    soft_start: float = 0.35,
    soft_end: float = 0.004,
    perturb: bool = True,
):
    """
    Local optimizer.  The sequence is maintained on the simplex (sum 1).
    The update is multiplicative:
        p_i <- p_i * exp(-lr * momentum(n*h_i - 1)),
    where h is a soft peak gradient from the convolution coefficients.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(123456789)

    p = _normalize(_clean_array(p0), 1.0).astype(np.float64).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, float("inf")
    if n == 1:
        return p, 2.0

    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    wlen = 2 * n - 1

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_sequence(p)

    if max_steps is None:
        if n <= 256:
            max_steps = 2000000
        elif n <= 512:
            max_steps = 1000000
        elif n <= 1024:
            max_steps = 500000
        else:
            max_steps = 250000

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    plan_time = max(1.0, float(deadline - t0))

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:wlen]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    m_mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.85

    lr_scale = 1.0
    bad_count = 0
    last_best_time = t0
    last_best_it = 0
    last_perturb_it = -1000000
    last_print = t0

    print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} start={best_score:.7f} budget={plan_time:.1f}s")

    for it in range(int(max_steps)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 0.4:
            break

        elapsed = now - t0
        progress_t = elapsed / plan_time if plan_time > 1e-12 else 1.0
        progress_i = (it + 1) / float(max_steps)
        progress = min(1.0, max(progress_t, progress_i))

        lr_base = (lr_end + 0.5 * (lr_start - lr_end) * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))) * lr_scale
        lr_base = max(1e-4, lr_base)
        soft = soft_end + (soft_start - soft_end) * (1.0 - progress)

        maxc = float(c.max())
        if (not np.isfinite(maxc)) or maxc <= 1e-300:
            break

        score = 2.0 * n * maxc
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = p.copy()
            last_best_time = now
            last_best_it = it
            if now - last_print > 5.0:
                print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} it={it} score={best_score:.7f} lr={lr_base:.4g} soft={soft:.4g}")
                last_print = now

        # Soft weights over convolution coefficients.
        T = soft * maxc
        if (not np.isfinite(T)) or T <= 1e-15 * maxc:
            w = np.zeros(wlen, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            ex = (c - maxc) / T
            ex = np.minimum(ex, 0.0)
            ex = np.maximum(ex, -50.0)
            w = np.exp(ex)
            ws = float(w.sum())
            if (not np.isfinite(ws)) or ws <= 1e-300:
                w = np.zeros(wlen, dtype=np.float64)
                w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            else:
                w = w / ws

        # Gradient h_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}, computed by FFT correlation.
        W = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        W[:wlen] = w
        H = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(W, L) * np.conj(P), L)
        h = H[:n].astype(np.float64)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        hs = float(h.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(hs)) or hs <= 1e-300:
            h = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            h = h / hs

        gdim = n * h - 1.0
        gdim = gdim - float(np.mean(gdim))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(gdim)):
            gdim = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        m_mom = b1 * m_mom + (1.0 - b1) * gdim
        bias = 1.0 - b1 ** (it + 1)
        if bias <= 1e-12:
            bias = 1e-12
        mhat = m_mom / bias
        mhat = np.clip(mhat, -3.0, 8.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(mhat)):
            mhat = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        exponent = -lr_base * mhat
        exponent = np.clip(exponent, -50.0, 50.0)
        q = p * np.exp(exponent)
        q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        s = float(q.sum())
        old_score = score

        if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
            q = best.copy()
            lr_scale = max(0.3, lr_scale * 0.5)
        else:
            q = q / s
            P = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
            c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:wlen]
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            nm = float(c.max())
            if not np.isfinite(nm):
                nm = 0.0
            new_score = 2.0 * n * nm
            p = q

            if new_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = new_score
                best = p.copy()
                last_best_time = time.monotonic()
                last_best_it = it
                if last_best_time - last_print > 5.0:
                    print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} it={it} score={best_score:.7f} lr={lr_base:.4g} soft={soft:.4g}")
                    last_print = last_best_time

            if new_score > old_score * 1.05 and new_score > old_score + 1e-9:
                bad_count += 1
            else:
                bad_count = max(0, bad_count - 1)

            if bad_count > 200:
                p = best.copy()
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
                c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:wlen]
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
                bad_count = 0
                lr_scale = max(0.4, lr_scale * 0.7)
                m_mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        # Simulated-anneal-like multiplicative perturbation after plateaus.
        if perturb:
            no_improve_it = it - last_best_it
            no_improve_t = time.monotonic() - last_best_time
            if (
                (it - last_perturb_it) >= max(1500, n)
                and (no_improve_it > max(2500, n) or no_improve_t > 8.0)
            ):
                if rng.random() < 0.80:
                    last_perturb_it = it
                    q2 = _perturb_mull(p, rng, sigma=0.45, frac=0.25)
                    qs2 = _score_sequence(q2)
                    cur_score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
                    accept = False
                    if qs2 < best_score * (1.0 + 1e-7):
                        accept = True
                    elif qs2 < cur_score * 1.15 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                        accept = True
                    if accept:
                        p = _normalize(q2, 1.0).copy()
                        P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
                        c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:wlen]
                        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
                        m_mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                        lr_scale = 1.0
                        bad_count = 0
                        print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} perturb accept score={qs2:.7f}")

    try:
        cur_score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
        if np.isfinite(cur_score) and cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best = p.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"[optimize {tag}] n={n} end best={best_score:.7f}")
    return best.copy(), best_score


def _pair_polish(p, deadline: float, rng, tag: str = ""):
    """
    Simple exact pair-transfer local search.  Move a random fraction of mass
    from a high-gradient index to a low-gradient index; accept if the exact
    objective improves.
    """
    p = _normalize(_clean_array(p), 1.0).astype(np.float64).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n < 8:
        return p.copy(), _score_sequence(p)

    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    wlen = 2 * n - 1
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_sequence(p)

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:wlen]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    trail = 0
    no_improve = 0
    src_count = max(16, n // 16)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.8:
        trail += 1
        if no_improve > 2500:
            break

        maxc = float(c.max())
        cur_score = 2.0 * n * maxc
        if (not np.isfinite(maxc)) or maxc <= 1e-300:
            break

        K = min(8, wlen)
        if K <= 0:
            break
        if K == wlen:
            top = np.arange(wlen)
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in top:
            k = int(kk)
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            if hi < lo:
                continue
            st = k - lo
            if hi == lo:
                h[lo] += p[st]
            else:
                end = k - hi
                if end == 0:
                    h[lo : hi + 1] += p[st::-1]
                else:
                    h[lo : hi + 1] += p[st:end - 1:-1]

        hs = float(h.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(hs)) or hs <= 1e-300:
            continue
        h = h / hs

        if n > 2 * src_count:
            src = np.argpartition(h, -src_count)[-src_count:]
            tgt = np.argpartition(h, src_count)[:src_count]
        else:
            src = np.argsort(h)[-src_count:]
            tgt = np.argsort(h)[:src_count]
        if src.size == 0 or tgt.size == 0:
            continue

        u = int(rng.choice(src))
        v = int(rng.choice(tgt))
        if u == v:
            continue

        base_move = p[u] * float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.55))
        improved = False
        for frac in (0.35, 1.0, 0.15, 0.55, 0.75):
            move = base_move * frac
            if move <= p[u] * 1e-12 or move > p[u]:
                continue
            q = p.copy()
            q[u] -= move
            q[v] += move
            q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
            qs = _score_sequence(q)
            if qs < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = q.copy()
                best_score = qs
                p = q
                P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
                c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, L)[:wlen]
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
                improved = True
                no_improve = 0
                break
        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1

        if trail % 250 == 0:
            print(f"[_pair_polish {tag}] n={n} trail={trail} best={best_score:.7f}")

    return best.copy(), best_score


def _make_candidates(base, rng):
    """
    Build an ordered list of (name, sequence, allocation_seconds).
    Base-derived candidates are prioritized; analytic seeds are included
    for exploration.
    """
    cands = []

    if base is not None:
        m = base.size
        if m >= 8:
            nbase = min(m, 2048)
            if m != nbase:
                pbase = _resample(base, nbase)
            else:
                pbase = _normalize(base.copy(), 1.0)

            cands.append((f"base{nbase}", pbase, 300.0))

            for nn, alloc in ((1024, 260.0), (2048, 220.0), (1536, 140.0), (768, 100.0)):
                if nn != nbase and nn <= 2048:
                    cands.append((f"base{nn}", _resample(base, nn), alloc))

            pjit = pbase * (1.0 + 0.15 * rng.normal(size=nbase))
            pjit = np.maximum(pjit, 0.0)
            pjit = _normalize(pjit, 1.0)
            cands.append(("basejit", pjit, 100.0))

            # A few non-base shapes to avoid overfitting the local basin.
            for n in (512, 1024):
                cands.append((f"altBeta{n}", _beta_sequence(n, 0.5, False), 80.0))
                cands.append((f"altBetaR{n}", _beta_sequence(n, 0.5, True), 50.0))
                cands.append((f"altStep{n}", _step_sequence(n, 0.5, 2.0, False), 50.0))
                cands.append((f"altNoise{n}", _smooth_noise_sequence(n, rng), 40.0))

    if base is None:
        for n in (512, 1024):
            cands.append((f"uni{n}", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), 120.0))
            for beta in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
                cands.append((f"beta{n}{beta}", _beta_sequence(n, beta, False), 80.0))
                cands.append((f"beta{n}r{beta}", _beta_sequence(n, beta, True), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"step{n}", _step_sequence(n, 0.5, 2.0, False), 70.0))
            cands.append((f"step{n}r", _step_sequence(n, 0.5, 1.5, True), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"gauss{n}l", _gauss_sequence(n, 0.25, 0.18), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"gauss{n}r", _gauss_sequence(n, 0.70, 0.18), 50.0))
            cands.append((f"noise{n}", _smooth_noise_sequence(n, rng), 50.0))

    return cands


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 2380.0
    budget_s = max(120.0, budget_s)

    total = budget_s - 10.0
    reserve = max(120.0, min(420.0, total * 0.22))
    exploration_end = t0 + total - reserve
    final_deadline = t0 + total - 2.0

    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    base = None
    try:
        candidates_global = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        arr = _clean_array(candidates_global)
        if arr.size >= 8:
            base = arr
    except Exception:
        base = None

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    cands = _make_candidates(base, rng)

    # Exploratory / local optimization pass on several candidates.
    for name, p0, alloc in cands:
        if time.monotonic() > exploration_end - 6.0:
            break
        rem = exploration_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 15.0:
            break

        alloc = min(float(alloc), rem)
        p0 = _normalize(p0, 1.0).astype(np.float64).copy()
        s0 = _score_sequence(p0)

        # Do not give long budgets to obviously poor starts after a good best.
        if np.isfinite(best_score) and (s0 - best_score) > 0.55:
            alloc = min(alloc, 30.0)
            if alloc < 8.0:
                continue

        print(f"Trying {name} | n={p0.size} | start={s0:.7f} | alloc={alloc:.1f}s")

        if s0 < best_score:
            best_score = s0
            best_seq = p0.copy()

        sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + alloc
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(p0, sub_deadline, tag=name, rng=rng)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print(f"New global best from {name}: {best_score:.7f} | n={best_seq.size}")
        else:
            print(f"{name} local_best={score:.7f} | global_best={best_score:.7f}")

    # If nothing has been found, use a safe fallback.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = _score_sequence(best_seq)
    else:
        best_seq = _normalize(best_seq, 1.0).astype(np.float64).copy()

    # Final refinement stages.
    remaining = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 90.0:
        d1 = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(330.0, remaining * 0.35))
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(
            best_seq,
            d1,
            tag="final-polish",
            rng=rng,
            lr_start=0.30,
            lr_end=0.006,
            soft_start=0.15,
            soft_end=0.0008,
            perturb=True,
        )
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print(f"Final polish improved to {best_score:.7f}")

    # Upsample the current best trial; longer lengths can reduce the discrete peak.
    n_best = best_seq.size
    if n_best < 2048 and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 90.0:
        nn = min(2048, n_best * 2)
        p_up = _resample(best_seq, nn)
        rem2 = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        d2 = time.monotonic() + max(30.0, min(300.0, rem2 * 0.45))
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(
            p_up,
            d2,
            tag=f"upscale-{nn}",
            rng=rng,
            lr_start=0.35,
            lr_end=0.008,
            soft_start=0.20,
            soft_end=0.001,
            perturb=True,
        )
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print(f"Upscale improved to {best_score:.7f} | n={best_seq.size}")
        else:
            print(f"Upscale n={nn} local={score:.7f} | global={best_score:.7f}")

    # Occasionally a slightly shorter length can have a better quantization.
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size > 1024 and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 70.0:
        nn = max(512, best_seq.size // 2)
        p_dn = _resample(best_seq, nn)
        rem3 = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        d3 = time.monotonic() + max(20.0, min(120.0, rem3 * 0.40))
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(
            p_dn,
            d3,
            tag=f"downscale-{nn}",
            rng=rng,
            lr_start=0.30,
            lr_end=0.006,
            soft_start=0.18,
            soft_end=0.001,
            perturb=True,
        )
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print(f"Downscale improved to {best_score:.7f} | n={best_seq.size}")
        else:
            print(f"Downscale n={nn} local={score:.7f} | global={best_score:.7f}")

    # LP-based best-response blending, useful for escaping shallow plateaus.
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 25.0:
        for _ in range(3):
            if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 20.0:
                break
            tl = max(3.0, min(10.0, (final_deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.18))
            g = _lp_best_response(best_seq, time_limit=tl)
            if g is None:
                break
            improved = False
            for mix in (0.06, 0.12, 0.22, 0.35, 0.55):
                q = (1.0 - mix) * best_seq + mix * g
                q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
                q = _normalize(q, 1.0)
                qs = _score_sequence(q)
                if np.isfinite(qs) and qs < best_score:
                    best_seq = q.copy()
                    best_score = qs
                    improved = True
                    print(f"LP blend m={mix:.2f} improved to {best_score:.7f}")
                    break
            if not improved:
                break

    # Pair-transfer polishing.
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 1500 and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 40.0:
        rem4 = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        d4 = time.monotonic() + max(20.0, min(80.0, rem4 * 0.30))
        seq, score = _pair_polish(best_seq, d4, rng, tag="pair")
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print(f"Pair polish improved to {best_score:.7f}")

    # Tiny final sharpening.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 18.0:
        rem5 = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        d5 = time.monotonic() + max(8.0, min(45.0, rem5 * 0.80))
        seq, score = optimize_sequence(
            best_seq,
            d5,
            tag="final2",
            rng=rng,
            lr_start=0.15,
            lr_end=0.003,
            soft_start=0.10,
            soft_end=0.0004,
            perturb=False,
        )
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_seq = seq.copy()
            print(f"final2 improved to {best_score:.7f}")

    # Produce the final non-negative normalized list.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

    n = best_seq.size
    target_sum = max(1.0, math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n)))
    final = _normalize(best_seq, target_sum)
    final = np.maximum(final, 0.0)
    # Make values strictly positive while preserving the objective to high precision.
    final = final + 1e-12
    final = _normalize(final, target_sum)
    final = np.minimum(final, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in final]

    exact = _score_exact(final)
    print(f"Final constructed score (exact local formula): {exact:.7f} | n={n}")

    try:
        ev = float(evaluate_sequence(out))  # noqa: F821
        if np.isfinite(ev):
            print(f"Final evaluate_sequence: {ev:.7f}")
        else:
            print("Final evaluate_sequence: invalid/inf (unexpected)")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if (not np.isfinite(exact)) or exact > 10.0 or len(out) == 0:
        fallback = _normalize(np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64), math.sqrt(1024.0))
        fallback = fallback + 1e-12
        fallback = _normalize(fallback, math.sqrt(1024.0))
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    return out
```
